## 1. Executive Summary

- 13 seed IOCs expanded into 18,918 Malanta cluster-sourced IoPAs across 22,069 graph nodes and 74,090 edges, indicating large-scale pre-attack infrastructure staging.
- Classification scope is material: 5,071 indicators labelled malicious and 2,423 suspicious; example malicious domains include `01eqyc.com`, `0bv2ga.com`, and `0o0o0o0o0.com` with p=1.0.
- Highest-risk clusters: `899AF20F-F08...` risk 97.9% / 330 domains, `196E7B73-CC1...` risk 97.5% / 448 domains, and `2562CA80-210...` risk 79.3% / 29 domains.
- APT signals are Medium confidence: Threat Group-3390 at 72% from code-repository evidence; El Machete and Comment Crew at 64%; Turla, APT28, Stealth Falcon, and Chinastrats at 62%.
- Hosting/ASN concentration cannot be assessed from the provided data; repository corroboration exists for 4 seed IPs across URLhaus and Unit42. Overall confidence: High for infrastructure clustering, Medium for attribution.

**Confidence:**
64.3%
 (source: malanta_api, 2 records)

**Indicators:**

`agaliarept.com`
Cluster

`blogwhereyou.com`
Cluster

**Known TTPs:** 
Spearphishing with weaponized documents and archives
Custom Machete malware for espionage collection
Screenshot, keystroke, clipboard, and file theft
Exfiltration over attacker-controlled web infrastructure

agaliarept.com: Malanta Threat-Intelligence Database matched this cluster-discovered indicator to El Machete.
blogwhereyou.com: Malanta Threat-Intelligence Database matched this cluster-discovered indicator to El Machete.
